					<div class="vector-body-before-content">
							<div class="mw-indicators">
		</div>

						<div id="siteSub" class="noprint">From Wikipedia, the free encyclopedia</div>
						<div id="contentSub"><div id="mw-content-subtitle"></div></div>
						
					</div>
					
					<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content"><div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr" data-mw-parsoid-version="0.24.0.0-alpha27" data-mw-html-version="2.8.0"><section data-mw-section-id="0" id="mwAQ"><div class="shortdescription nomobile noexcerpt noprint searchaux" style="display:none" about="#mwt1" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"short description","href":"./Template:Short_description"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Randomized algorithm"}},"i":0}}]}'>Randomized algorithm</div><span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt1"><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_short_description"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Short_description_matches_Wikidata"/></span>
<p id="mwAw"><b id="mwBA">Reservoir sampling</b> is a family of randomized <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Online_algorithm" title="Online algorithm" id="mwBQ">online algorithms</a> for choosing a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Simple_random_sample" title="Simple random sample" id="mwBg">simple random sample</a>, without replacement, of <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt2" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"k"}},"i":0}}]}'>k</span> items from a population of unknown size <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt3" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwCA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"n"}},"i":0}}]}'>n</span> in a single pass over the items.  The size of the population <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt4" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwCQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"n"}},"i":0}}]}'>n</span> is not known to the algorithm and is typically too large for all <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt5" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwCg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"n"}},"i":0}}]}'>n</span> items to fit into <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Main_memory" title="Main memory" class="mw-redirect" id="mwCw">main memory</a>.  The population is revealed to the algorithm over time, and the algorithm cannot look back at previous items. At any point, the current state of the algorithm must permit extraction of a simple random sample without replacement of size <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt6" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwDA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"k"}},"i":0}}]}'>k</span> over the part of the population seen so far.</p>

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwDQ" data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}'/></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwDg" aria-labelledby="Example"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Example">Example</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Example"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwDw">Consider the case when we want to maintain a sample of one item. A simple algorithm is to store the first item with probability <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt7" id="mwEA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"1"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle 1}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mn>1</mn>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle 1}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/92d98b82a3778f043108d4e20960a9193df57cbf" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.162ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle 1}"/></span> and the <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt8" id="mwEQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>k</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c3c9a2c7b599b37105512c5d570edc034056dd40" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.211ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k}"/></span>-th item with probability <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt9" id="mwEg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\frac{1}{k}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle {\frac {1}{k}}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mfrac>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mfrac>
        </mrow>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle {\frac {1}{k}}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/0e8560804e21ad155b4f9d8e8a8acd6fae698bb6" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -2.005ex; width:2.047ex; height:5.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\frac {1}{k}}}"/></span>. We want to show that the sample is uniformly distributed over all of the items that we have seen at each step since.<sup about="#mwt12" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-1" id="mwEw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwFA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFQ">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwFw">The proof is by induction on the number of items <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt13" id="mwGA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>n</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a601995d55609f2d9f5e233e36fbe9ea26011b3b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.395ex; height:1.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n}"/></span>. If there is 1 item we always choose it and we are done. If there are already <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt14" id="mwGQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>n</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a601995d55609f2d9f5e233e36fbe9ea26011b3b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.395ex; height:1.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n}"/></span> items, then the probability of one of the items already in the store remaining the store becomes <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt15" id="mwGg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\frac{n}{n + 1} \\times \\frac{1}{n} = \\frac{1}{n + 1}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle {\frac {n}{n+1}}\times {\frac {1}{n}}={\frac {1}{n+1}}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mfrac>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mrow>
              <mi>n</mi>
              <mo>+</mo>
              <mn>1</mn>
            </mrow>
          </mfrac>
        </mrow>
        <mo>×<!-- × --></mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mfrac>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mi>n</mi>
          </mfrac>
        </mrow>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mfrac>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mrow>
              <mi>n</mi>
              <mo>+</mo>
              <mn>1</mn>
            </mrow>
          </mfrac>
        </mrow>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle {\frac {n}{n+1}}\times {\frac {1}{n}}={\frac {1}{n+1}}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/9342fc193e476788ea9a75c7d0781c1e53b1b245" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -2.005ex; width:20.637ex; height:5.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\frac {n}{n+1}}\times {\frac {1}{n}}={\frac {1}{n+1}}}"/></span> so the probability of any of the <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt16" id="mwGw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n + 1"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n+1}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n+1}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/2a135e65a42f2d73cccbfc4569523996ca0036f1" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.505ex; width:5.398ex; height:2.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n+1}"/></span> items remaining in the sample is now <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt17" id="mwHA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\frac{1}{n + 1}."}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle {\frac {1}{n+1}}.}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mfrac>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mrow>
              <mi>n</mi>
              <mo>+</mo>
              <mn>1</mn>
            </mrow>
          </mfrac>
        </mrow>
        <mo>.</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle {\frac {1}{n+1}}.}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a9ec509a760a0e4284f214b2c34b4f35cbf4273d" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -2.005ex; width:6.881ex; height:5.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\frac {1}{n+1}}.}"/></span> The proof of correctness for the general algorithm where we have an arbitrary number of slots is an extension of this argument. </p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwHQ" aria-labelledby="History_and_Algorithm_R"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="History_and_Algorithm_R">History and Algorithm R</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: History and Algorithm R"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwHg">Reservoir sampling was introduced as early as 1962 by Fan, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mervin_E._Muller" title="Mervin E. Muller" id="mwHw">Muller</a>, and Rezucha, who described a sampling algorithm which did not need to know the population size <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt18" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwIA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"n"}},"i":0}}]}'>n</span> in advance.<sup about="#mwt21" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-fmr1962_2-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"fmr1962"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-fmr1962-2"}}'><a href="#cite_note-fmr1962-2" id="mwIQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwIg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIw">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJA">]</span></span></a></sup>
A simpler algorithm, known as Algorithm R, was discovered independently by Alan G. Waterman<sup about="#mwt24" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-3"}}'><a href="#cite_note-3" id="mwJQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwJg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJw">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt28" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-vitter_4-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"vitter"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-vitter-4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-vitter-4" id="mwKQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwKg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwLA">]</span></span></a></sup>
and McLeod <span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwLQ">&amp;</span> Bellhouse;<sup about="#mwt31" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-mb1983_5-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"mb1983"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-mb1983-5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-mb1983-5" id="mwLg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwLw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwMA">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwMQ">]</span></span></a></sup>
it can also be viewed as a variant of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fisher–Yates_shuffle" title="Fisher–Yates shuffle" id="mwMg">Fisher–Yates shuffle</a>.<sup about="#mwt34" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-6" id="mwMw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwNA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNQ">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNg">]</span></span></a></sup>  Algorithm R works as follows.</p>

<ul id="mwNw"><li id="mwOA">Initialize an array <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt36" id="mwOQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"R"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle R}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>R</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle R}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/4b0bfb3769bf24d80e15374dc37b0441e2616e33" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.764ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle R}"/></span> indexed from <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt37" id="mwOg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"1"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle 1}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mn>1</mn>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle 1}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/92d98b82a3778f043108d4e20960a9193df57cbf" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.162ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle 1}"/></span> to <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt38" id="mwOw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>k</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c3c9a2c7b599b37105512c5d570edc034056dd40" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.211ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k}"/></span>, containing the first <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt35" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwPA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"k"}},"i":0}}]}'>k</span> items of the input <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt39" id="mwPQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"x_1, ..., x_k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle x_{1},...,x_{k}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>x</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <mo>.</mo>
        <mo>.</mo>
        <mo>.</mo>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <msub>
          <mi>x</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle x_{1},...,x_{k}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/2b83bf0a25e29a5395cc534f35edc8fa7e2d18ca" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:9.972ex; height:2.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle x_{1},...,x_{k}}"/></span>. This is the <i id="mwPg">reservoir</i>.</li>
<li id="mwPw">For each new input <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt41" id="mwQA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"x_i"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle x_{i}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>x</mi>

<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt47" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwSA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Proof ","href":"./Template:Proof"},"params":{"title":{"wt":"Proof of correctness of Algorithm R"},"proof":{"wt":"The correctness proof shows by induction on {{mvar|i}} that after the {{mvar|i}}&apos;th step of the algorithm, the reservoir contains a uniformly random sample of the inputs &lt;math>x_1, \\ldots, x_i&lt;/math>.\n\nWhen &lt;math>i = k&lt;/math>, Algorithm R returns all inputs, so this is the base case of the induction.\n\nFor the inductive step, it is given that just before the &lt;math>(i+1)&lt;/math>-th input is processed, the reservoir forms a uniformly random sample of &lt;math>x_1, \\ldots, x_i&lt;/math>.  We must show that just after the &lt;math>(i+1)&lt;/math>-th input is processed, the reservoir forms a uniformly random sample of &lt;math>x_1, \\dots, x_{i+1}&lt;/math>.\n\n\nApply Algorithm R to the &lt;math>(i+1)&lt;/math>-th input. Let &lt;math>S&lt;/math> be any subset of size &lt;math>k&lt;/math> among the first &lt;math>i+1&lt;/math> inputs. By the inductive hypothesis, every subset of size &lt;math>k&lt;/math> among the first &lt;math>i&lt;/math> inputs has probability &lt;math>1/\\binom{i}{k}&lt;/math> of being the reservoir.\n\nWe consider two cases.\n\n&apos;&apos;&apos;Case 1:&apos;&apos;&apos; &lt;math>x_{i+1}\\notin S&lt;/math>.\n\nFor &lt;math>S&lt;/math> to be the reservoir after this step, it must have been the previous reservoir, and the new input must not be inserted. Thus,\n\n&lt;math>\nP(R_{i+1}=S)\n=\\frac{1}{\\binom{i}{k}}\\left(1-\\frac{k}{i+1}\\right)\n=\\frac{k!(i+1-k)!}{(i+1)!}\n=\\frac{1}{\\binom{i+1}{k}}.\n&lt;/math>\n\n&apos;&apos;&apos;Case 2:&apos;&apos;&apos; &lt;math>x_{i+1}\\in S&lt;/math>.\n\nThe previous reservoir must have contained the other &lt;math>k-1&lt;/math> elements of &lt;math>S&lt;/math>, together with one additional element &lt;math>y\\notin S&lt;/math>. The new input must replace &lt;math>y&lt;/math>.\n\nThere are exactly &lt;math>i-k+1&lt;/math> possible choices for &lt;math>y&lt;/math>. Each corresponding previous reservoir has probability &lt;math>1/\\binom{i}{k}&lt;/math>, and the probability of replacing the particular element &lt;math>y&lt;/math> is &lt;math>1/(i+1)&lt;/math>. Since these possibilities are mutually exclusive,\n\n&lt;math>\nP(R_{i+1}=S)\n=\\frac{i-k+1}{(i+1)\\binom{i}{k}}\n=\\frac{k!(i+1-k)!}{(i+1)!}\n=\\frac{1}{\\binom{i+1}{k}}.\n&lt;/math>\n\nTherefore, in both cases, every subset of size &lt;math>k&lt;/math> has probability &lt;math>1/\\binom{i+1}{k}&lt;/math> of being the reservoir. By induction, Algorithm R produces a uniformly random subset of size &lt;math>k&lt;/math> from the inputs."}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1174254338" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt48" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Math_proof/styles.css"}}'>.mw-parser-output .math_proof{border:thin solid #aaa;margin:1em 2em;padding:0.5em 1em 0.4em}@media(max-width:500px){.mw-parser-output .math_proof{margin:1em 0;padding:0.5em 0.5em 0.4em}}</style></span><div class="math_proof" style="" about="#mwt47" id="mwSQ"><strong>Proof of correctness of Algorithm R</strong>
<p>The correctness proof shows by induction on <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;">i</span> that after the <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;">i</span>'th step of the algorithm, the reservoir contains a uniformly random sample of the inputs <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt49" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"x_1, \\ldots, x_i"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle x_{1},\ldots ,x_{i}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>x</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <mo>…<!-- … --></mo>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <msub>
          <mi>x</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>i</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle x_{1},\ldots ,x_{i}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/f279c1c7741cd865b81f65ce4680ed1c26fcd1a2" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:9.691ex; height:2.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle x_{1},\ldots ,x_{i}}"/></span>.</p>

Let <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt56" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"S"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S}">

By the inductive hypothesis, every subset of size <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt59" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k}">

<p>There are exactly <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt70" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"i-k+1"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle i-k+1}">

        </mrow>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mfrac>
            <mrow>
              <mi>k</mi>
              <mo>!</mo>
              <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
              <mi>i</mi>
              <mo>+</mo>
              <mn>1</mn>
              <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
              <mi>k</mi>
              <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
              <mo>!</mo>
            </mrow>
            <mrow>
              <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
              <mi>i</mi>
              <mo>+</mo>
              <mn>1</mn>
              <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
              <mo>!</mo>
            </mrow>
          </mfrac>
        </mrow>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mfrac>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mrow>
              <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-OPEN">
                <mo maxsize="1.2em" minsize="1.2em">(</mo>
              </mrow>
              <mfrac linethickness="0">
                <mrow>
                  <mi>i</mi>
                  <mo>+</mo>
                  <mn>1</mn>
                </mrow>
                <mi>k</mi>
              </mfrac>
              <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-CLOSE">
                <mo maxsize="1.2em" minsize="1.2em">)</mo>
              </mrow>
            </mrow>
          </mfrac>
        </mrow>
        <mo>.</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle P(R_{i+1}=S)={\frac {i-k+1}{(i+1){\binom {i}{k}}}}={\frac {k!(i+1-k)!}{(i+1)!}}={\frac {1}{\binom {i+1}{k}}}.}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/9c1d7e4eb3c0529a89c2dee2bc84694be29d276b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -3.171ex; width:52.837ex; height:7.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle P(R_{i+1}=S)={\frac {i-k+1}{(i+1){\binom {i}{k}}}}={\frac {k!(i+1-k)!}{(i+1)!}}={\frac {1}{\binom {i+1}{k}}}.}"/></span></p>

<p>Therefore, in both cases, every subset of size <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt76" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>k</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c3c9a2c7b599b37105512c5d570edc034056dd40" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.211ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k}"/></span> has probability <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt77" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"1/\\binom{i+1}{k}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle 1/{\binom {i+1}{k}}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mo>/</mo>
        </mrow>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mrow>
            <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-OPEN">
              <mo maxsize="2.047em" minsize="2.047em">(</mo>
            </mrow>
            <mfrac linethickness="0">
              <mrow>
                <mi>i</mi>
                <mo>+</mo>
                <mn>1</mn>
              </mrow>
              <mi>k</mi>
            </mfrac>
            <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-CLOSE">
              <mo maxsize="2.047em" minsize="2.047em">)</mo>
            </mrow>
          </mrow>
        </mrow>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle 1/{\binom {i+1}{k}}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/0008b1d8fa2c26db42653be581ae57223a91bb1b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -2.505ex; width:10.551ex; height:6.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle 1/{\binom {i+1}{k}}}"/></span> of being the reservoir. By induction, Algorithm R produces a uniformly random subset of size <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt78" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>k</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c3c9a2c7b599b37105512c5d570edc034056dd40" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.211ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k}"/></span> from the inputs.</p>
</div>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwSg" aria-labelledby="Optimizing_running_time_by_skipping_elements"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Optimizing_running_time_by_skipping_elements">Optimizing running time by skipping elements</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Optimizing running time by skipping elements"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwSw">The Algorithm R described in the previous section has linear runtime, since it generates a random number for every element in the input stream to determine whether it should be included in the reservoir.  It was observed by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeffrey_Vitter" title="Jeffrey Vitter" id="mwTA">Jeffrey Vitter</a> that it is possible to obtain faster algorithms with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sublinear_algorithm" title="Sublinear algorithm" class="mw-redirect" id="mwTQ">sublinear</a> runtime when the algorithm is allowed to skip past sections of the input stream without examining them.  Vitter gave a reservoir sampling algorithm which runs in time <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt79" id="mwTg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(k(1 + \\log(n/k))"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(k(1+\log(n/k))}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>k</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>log</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mo>/</mo>
        </mrow>
        <mi>k</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle O(k(1+\log(n/k))}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/5fd10f1717ed42e29bd346dc0faf301ae4fd5de0" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:18.251ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle O(k(1+\log(n/k))}"/></span>, and proved that this is optimal.<sup about="#mwt82" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-vitter84_7-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"vitter84"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-vitter84-7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-vitter84-7" id="mwTw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwUA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUQ">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt83" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-vitter_4-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"vitter"}}'><a href="#cite_note-vitter-4" id="mwUw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwVA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVQ">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVg">]</span></span></a></sup>
Kim-Hung Li described a simple algorithm, Algorithm L, achieving the same asymptotic runtime, and which is described in this section.<sup about="#mwt86" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-li_8-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"li"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-li-8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-li-8" id="mwVw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwWA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWQ">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwWw">The starting point for Algorithm L is the observation that if <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt87" id="mwXA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>n</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a601995d55609f2d9f5e233e36fbe9ea26011b3b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.395ex; height:1.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n}"/></span> random numbers <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt88" id="mwXQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"u_1, ..., u_n\\sim U[0, 1]"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle u_{1},...,u_{n}\sim U[0,1]}">

        <mo>.</mo>

          </mrow>

        <mi>U</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
        <mn>0</mn>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle u_{1},...,u_{n}\sim U[0,1]}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/f594c6c9844a93042bad13e86244913ed9b819de" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:19.635ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle u_{1},...,u_{n}\sim U[0,1]}"/></span> are generated uniformly and independently, then the indices of the smallest <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt89" id="mwXg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k}">

      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>k</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c3c9a2c7b599b37105512c5d570edc034056dd40" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.211ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k}"/></span> of them form a uniform sample of the <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt90" id="mwXw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>k</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c3c9a2c7b599b37105512c5d570edc034056dd40" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.211ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k}"/></span>-subsets of <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt91" id="mwYA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\{1, ..., n\\}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \{1,...,n\}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mo fence="false" stretchy="false">{</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo>,</mo>

        <mo>.</mo>
        <mo>.</mo>

        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo fence="false" stretchy="false">}</mo>

The process can be done without knowing <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt92" id="mwYg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n}">
  <semantics>

      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>n</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a601995d55609f2d9f5e233e36fbe9ea26011b3b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.395ex; height:1.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n}"/></span>: </p><blockquote id="mwYw"><p id="mwZA">Keep the smallest <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt93" id="mwZQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k}">

  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">

          </mrow>

  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/25e072cd1b695907a3255dcb07a1d2fb8ed870c3" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:9.683ex; height:2.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle u_{1},...,u_{i}}"/></span> that has been seen so far, as well as <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt95" id="mwZw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"w_i "}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle w_{i}}">

For each new <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt96" id="mwaA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"u_{i+1}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle u_{i+1}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">

  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>u</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <msub>

              <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
                <mi>i</mi>
              </mrow>
            </msub>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>

<p id="mwdg">This algorithm still needs <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt109" id="mwdw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(n)}">

    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle O(n)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/34109fe397fdcff370079185bfdb65826cb5565a" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.977ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle O(n)}"/></span> random numbers, thus taking <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt110" id="mweA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(n)}">
  <semantics>

    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle O(n)}</annotation>
