</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/34109fe397fdcff370079185bfdb65826cb5565a" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.977ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle O(n)}"/></span> time.  However, it can be improved by observing that after each step, the number of following items which will be skipped by the algorithm follows a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Geometric_distribution" title="Geometric distribution" id="mweQ">geometric distribution</a> with parameter <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt111" id="mweg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"u_{w_i}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle u_{w_{i}}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>u</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <msub>
              <mi>w</mi>
              <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
                <mi>i</mi>
              </mrow>
            </msub>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle u_{w_{i}}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a8f8edf8a397febeb03c7897d24fc9c9a74e3cd2" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -1.005ex; width:3.364ex; height:2.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle u_{w_{i}}}"/></span>.  Thus, the sampling algorithm can simply generate a sample from this distribution to determine how many items to skip over, rather than examining them one at a time.  A further simplification is to store only the maximum value <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt112" id="mwew" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"u_{w_i}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle u_{w_{i}}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>u</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <msub>
              <mi>w</mi>
              <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
                <mi>i</mi>
              </mrow>
            </msub>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle u_{w_{i}}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a8f8edf8a397febeb03c7897d24fc9c9a74e3cd2" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -1.005ex; width:3.364ex; height:2.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle u_{w_{i}}}"/></span> at each point, and avoid storing <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt113" id="mwfA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"u_1, \\ldots, u_i"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle u_{1},\ldots ,u_{i}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>u</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <mo>…<!-- … --></mo>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <msub>
          <mi>u</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>i</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle u_{1},\ldots ,u_{i}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/fb6518ebb270bee29bd7f8ca1043fcd8bd4c4499" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:9.691ex; height:2.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle u_{1},\ldots ,u_{i}}"/></span>.  This results in Algorithm L, which is implemented as follows:</p>

<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-pascal mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt114" id="mwfQ" data-mw='{"name":"syntaxhighlight","attrs":{"lang":"pascal"},"body":{"extsrc":"\n// S has items to sample, R will contain the result\nReservoirSample(S[1..n], R[1..k])\n  // fill the reservoir array with the first k items\n  for i = 1 to k\n      R[i] := S[i]\n  end\n\n  // random() generates a uniform (0,1) random number\n  W := exp(log(random())/k)\n\n  while i &lt;= n\n      // skip items according to a geometric distribution\n      i := i + floor(log(random())/log(1-W)) + 1\n      if i &lt;= n\n          // replace a random item of the reservoir with item i\n          R[randomInteger(1,k)] := S[i]\n          W := W * exp(log(random())/k)\n      end\n  end\nend\n"}}'><pre id="mwfg"><span id="mwfw"></span><span class="c1" id="mwgA">// S has items to sample, R will contain the result</span>
<span class="n" id="mwgQ">ReservoirSample</span><span class="p" id="mwgg">(</span><span class="n" id="mwgw">S</span><span class="p" id="mwhA">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwhQ">1</span><span class="o" id="mwhg">..</span><span class="n" id="mwhw">n</span><span class="p" id="mwiA">]</span><span class="o" id="mwiQ">,</span><span class="w" id="mwig"> </span><span class="n" id="mwiw">R</span><span class="p" id="mwjA">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwjQ">1</span><span class="o" id="mwjg">..</span><span class="n" id="mwjw">k</span><span class="p" id="mwkA">])</span>
<span class="w" id="mwkQ">  </span><span class="c1" id="mwkg">// fill the reservoir array with the first k items</span>
<span class="w" id="mwkw">  </span><span class="k" id="mwlA">for</span><span class="w" id="mwlQ"> </span><span class="n" id="mwlg">i</span><span class="w" id="mwlw"> </span><span class="o" id="mwmA">=</span><span class="w" id="mwmQ"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwmg">1</span><span class="w" id="mwmw"> </span><span class="k" id="mwnA">to</span><span class="w" id="mwnQ"> </span><span class="n" id="mwng">k</span>
<span class="w" id="mwnw">      </span><span class="n" id="mwoA">R</span><span class="p" id="mwoQ">[</span><span class="n" id="mwog">i</span><span class="p" id="mwow">]</span><span class="w" id="mwpA"> </span><span class="o" id="mwpQ">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwpg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwpw">S</span><span class="p" id="mwqA">[</span><span class="n" id="mwqQ">i</span><span class="p" id="mwqg">]</span>
<span class="w" id="mwqw">  </span><span class="k" id="mwrA">end</span>

<span class="w" id="mwrQ">  </span><span class="c1" id="mwrg">// random() generates a uniform (0,1) random number</span>
<span class="w" id="mwrw">  </span><span class="n" id="mwsA">W</span><span class="w" id="mwsQ"> </span><span class="o" id="mwsg">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwsw"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwtA">exp</span><span class="p" id="mwtQ">(</span><span class="n" id="mwtg">log</span><span class="p" id="mwtw">(</span><span class="nb" id="mwuA">random</span><span class="p" id="mwuQ">())</span><span class="o" id="mwug">/</span><span class="n" id="mwuw">k</span><span class="p" id="mwvA">)</span>

<span class="w" id="mwvQ">  </span><span class="k" id="mwvg">while</span><span class="w" id="mwvw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwwA">i</span><span class="w" id="mwwQ"> </span><span class="o" id="mwwg">&lt;=</span><span class="w" id="mwww"> </span><span class="n" id="mwxA">n</span>
<span class="w" id="mwxQ">      </span><span class="c1" id="mwxg">// skip items according to a geometric distribution</span>
<span class="w" id="mwxw">      </span><span class="n" id="mwyA">i</span><span class="w" id="mwyQ"> </span><span class="o" id="mwyg">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwyw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwzA">i</span><span class="w" id="mwzQ"> </span><span class="o" id="mwzg">+</span><span class="w" id="mwzw"> </span><span class="nb" id="mw0A">floor</span><span class="p" id="mw0Q">(</span><span class="n" id="mw0g">log</span><span class="p" id="mw0w">(</span><span class="nb" id="mw1A">random</span><span class="p" id="mw1Q">())</span><span class="o" id="mw1g">/</span><span class="n" id="mw1w">log</span><span class="p" id="mw2A">(</span><span class="mi" id="mw2Q">1</span><span class="o" id="mw2g">-</span><span class="n" id="mw2w">W</span><span class="p" id="mw3A">))</span><span class="w" id="mw3Q"> </span><span class="o" id="mw3g">+</span><span class="w" id="mw3w"> </span><span class="mi" id="mw4A">1</span>
<span class="w" id="mw4Q">      </span><span class="k" id="mw4g">if</span><span class="w" id="mw4w"> </span><span class="n" id="mw5A">i</span><span class="w" id="mw5Q"> </span><span class="o" id="mw5g">&lt;=</span><span class="w" id="mw5w"> </span><span class="n" id="mw6A">n</span>
<span class="w" id="mw6Q">          </span><span class="c1" id="mw6g">// replace a random item of the reservoir with item i</span>
<span class="w" id="mw6w">          </span><span class="n" id="mw7A">R</span><span class="p" id="mw7Q">[</span><span class="n" id="mw7g">randomInteger</span><span class="p" id="mw7w">(</span><span class="mi" id="mw8A">1</span><span class="o" id="mw8Q">,</span><span class="n" id="mw8g">k</span><span class="p" id="mw8w">)]</span><span class="w" id="mw9A"> </span><span class="o" id="mw9Q">:=</span><span class="w" id="mw9g"> </span><span class="n" id="mw9w">S</span><span class="p" id="mw-A">[</span><span class="n" id="mw-Q">i</span><span class="p" id="mw-g">]</span>
<span class="w" id="mw-w">          </span><span class="n" id="mw_A">W</span><span class="w" id="mw_Q"> </span><span class="o" id="mw_g">:=</span><span class="w" id="mw_w"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAQA">W</span><span class="w" id="mwAQE"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAQI">*</span><span class="w" id="mwAQM"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwAQQ">exp</span><span class="p" id="mwAQU">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAQY">log</span><span class="p" id="mwAQc">(</span><span class="nb" id="mwAQg">random</span><span class="p" id="mwAQk">())</span><span class="o" id="mwAQo">/</span><span class="n" id="mwAQs">k</span><span class="p" id="mwAQw">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAQ0">      </span><span class="k" id="mwAQ4">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAQ8">  </span><span class="k" id="mwARA">end</span>
<span class="k" id="mwARE">end</span>
</pre></div>

<p id="mwARI">This algorithm computes three random numbers for each item that becomes part of the reservoir, and does not spend any time on items that do not. Its expected running time is <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt115" id="mwARM" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(k (1+\\log(n/k)))"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(k(1+\log(n/k)))}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>k</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>log</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mo>/</mo>
        </mrow>
        <mi>k</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle O(k(1+\log(n/k)))}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/297e6733c834935521a0400e11e9d9817d9eb500" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:19.155ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle O(k(1+\log(n/k)))}"/></span>, which is optimal.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mwARQ" aria-labelledby="Sampling_with_replacement_and_the_single-item_case"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Sampling_with_replacement_and_the_single-item_case">Sampling with replacement and the single-item case</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Sampling with replacement and the single-item case"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwARU">Reservoir sampling can also be performed <b id="mwARY">with replacement</b>, meaning that the same input item may appear more than once in the final sample. In sampling without replacement, each input item can appear at most once. For a sample of size one, however, the resulting sample distribution is the same.</p>

<p id="mwARc">Park, Ostrouchov, Samatova, and Geist (2004) derived a skip-based algorithm for reservoir sampling with replacement.<sup about="#mwt118" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-9" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-9" id="mwARg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARo">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARs">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwARw">For a reservoir of size one, suppose the currently selected item is at 1-based position <span class="texhtml " about="#mwt119" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAR0" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"math","href":"./Template:Math"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"n"}},"i":0}}]}'>n</span> in the stream. The position of the next replacement can be sampled directly from a single uniform random value <span class="texhtml " about="#mwt120" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAR4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"math","href":"./Template:Math"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"q"}},"i":0}}]}'>q</span>:</p>

<dl id="mwAR8"><dd id="mwASA"><span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt121" id="mwASE" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n&apos; = \\left\\lfloor \\frac{n}{q} \\right\\rfloor + 1."}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n'=\left\lfloor {\frac {n}{q}}\right\rfloor +1.}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msup>
          <mi>n</mi>
          <mo>′</mo>
        </msup>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mrow>
          <mo>⌊</mo>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mfrac>
              <mi>n</mi>
              <mi>q</mi>
            </mfrac>
          </mrow>
          <mo>⌋</mo>
        </mrow>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mn>1.</mn>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n'=\left\lfloor {\frac {n}{q}}\right\rfloor +1.}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/2dc478838328508b62cbec66a52c368024d8c01c" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -2.505ex; width:14.769ex; height:6.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n'=\left\lfloor {\frac {n}{q}}\right\rfloor +1.}"/></span></dd></dl>

<p id="mwASI">This gives a particularly simple algorithm:</p>

<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-text mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt122" id="mwASM" data-mw='{"name":"syntaxhighlight","attrs":{"lang":"text"},"body":{"extsrc":"\nif stream is empty\n    return no sample\n\nsample := first item\nn := 1\n\nloop\n    q := random()   // uniform in (0,1)\n    n := floor(n / q) + 1\n\n    advance stream to 1-based item n\n    if stream is exhausted\n        break\n\n    sample := item n\n\nreturn sample\n"}}'><pre id="mwASQ"><span id="mwASU"></span>if stream is empty
    return no sample

sample := first item
n := 1

loop
    q := random()   // uniform in (0,1)
    n := floor(n / q) + 1

    advance stream to 1-based item n
    if stream is exhausted
        break

    sample := item n

return sample
</pre></div>

<p id="mwASY">Here <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-text mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt123" id="mwASc" data-mw='{"name":"syntaxhighlight","attrs":{"lang":"text","class":"","style":"","inline":"1"},"body":{"extsrc":"random()"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"code","href":"./Template:Code"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"random()"}},"i":0}}]}'>random()</code> denotes a uniform random value strictly between 0 and 1. An implementation whose random-number generator can return an endpoint must exclude or resample such values. The variable <span class="texhtml " about="#mwt125" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwASg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"math","href":"./Template:Math"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"n"}},"i":0}}]}'>n</span> is the absolute 1-based position in the stream, rather than a count of items to skip. The algorithm therefore generates replacement positions directly instead of making a random replacement decision for every input item.</p>

<p id="mwASk">For a reservoir of size one, each new replacement position requires only one random variate. By comparison, Algorithm L normally uses three. When Algorithm L is specialized to <span class="texhtml " about="#mwt126" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwASo" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"math","href":"./Template:Math"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"k = 1"}},"i":0}}]}'>k = 1</span>, choosing the reservoir slot requires no randomness, leaving two nontrivial random variates per replacement.<sup about="#mwt127" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-li_8-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"li"}}'><a href="#cite_note-li-8" id="mwASs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwASw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAS0">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAS4">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAS8">A weighted generalization was later given by Meligrana and Fazzone.<sup about="#mwt130" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-10" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-10" id="mwATA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwATE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATI">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwATQ">For a single-item weighted reservoir with current cumulative weight <span class="texhtml " about="#mwt131" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwATU" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"math","href":"./Template:Math"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"W"}},"i":0}}]}'>W</span>, the cumulative-weight threshold for the next replacement is</p>

<dl id="mwATY"><dd id="mwATc"><span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt132" id="mwATg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\frac{W}{q},"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle {\frac {W}{q}},}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mfrac>
            <mi>W</mi>
            <mi>q</mi>
          </mfrac>
        </mrow>
        <mo>,</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle {\frac {W}{q}},}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/0ea7e08190dba03001c76cb3d4deb24604814a5d" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -2.338ex; width:3.918ex; height:5.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\frac {W}{q}},}"/></span></dd></dl>

<p id="mwATk">where <span class="texhtml " about="#mwt133" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwATo" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"math","href":"./Template:Math"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"q"}},"i":0}}]}'>q</span> is uniform on <span class="texhtml " about="#mwt134" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwATs" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"math","href":"./Template:Math"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"(0,1)"}},"i":0}}]}'>(0,1)</span>. The stream is scanned until its cumulative weight reaches that threshold.</p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="5" id="mwATw" aria-labelledby="With_random_sort"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="With_random_sort">With random sort</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: With random sort"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwAT0">If we associate with each item of the input a uniformly generated random number, the <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt135" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAT4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"k"}},"i":0}}]}'>k</span> items with the largest (or, equivalently, smallest) associated values form a simple random sample.<sup about="#mwt139" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-fan_11-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"fan"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-fan-11"}}'><a href="#cite_note-fan-11" id="mwAT8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAUA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUE">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUI">]</span></span></a></sup>  A simple reservoir-sampling thus maintains the <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt136" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAUM" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"k"}},"i":0}}]}'>k</span> items with the currently largest associated values in a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Priority_queue" title="Priority queue" id="mwAUQ">priority queue</a>.</p>

<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-pascal mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt140" id="mwAUU" data-mw='{"name":"syntaxhighlight","attrs":{"lang":"pascal"},"body":{"extsrc":"\n(*\n  S is a stream of items to sample\n  S.Current returns current item in stream\n  S.Next advances stream to next position\n  min-priority-queue supports:\n    Count -> number of items in priority queue\n    Minimum -> returns minimum key value of all items\n    Extract-Min() -> Remove the item with minimum key\n    Insert(key, Item) -> Adds item with specified key\n *)\nReservoirSample(S[1..?])\n  H := new min-priority-queue\n  while S has data\n    r := random()   // uniformly random between 0 and 1, exclusive\n    if H.Count &lt; k\n      H.Insert(r, S.Current)\n    else\n      // keep k items with largest associated keys\n      if r > H.Minimum\n        H.Extract-Min()\n        H.Insert(r, S.Current)\n    end\n    S.Next\n  end\n  return items in H\nend\n"}}'><pre id="mwAUY"><span id="mwAUc"></span><span class="cm" id="mwAUg">(*</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAUk">  S is a stream of items to sample</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAUo">  S.Current returns current item in stream</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAUs">  S.Next advances stream to next position</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAUw">  min-priority-queue supports:</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAU0">    Count -> number of items in priority queue</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAU4">    Minimum -> returns minimum key value of all items</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAU8">    Extract-Min() -> Remove the item with minimum key</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAVA">    Insert(key, Item) -> Adds item with specified key</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAVE"> *)</span>
<span class="n" id="mwAVI">ReservoirSample</span><span class="p" id="mwAVM">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAVQ">S</span><span class="p" id="mwAVU">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwAVY">1</span><span class="o" id="mwAVc">..</span><span class="err" id="mwAVg">?</span><span class="p" id="mwAVk">])</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAVo">  </span><span class="n" id="mwAVs">H</span><span class="w" id="mwAVw"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAV0">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwAV4"> </span><span class="k" id="mwAV8">new</span><span class="w" id="mwAWA"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwAWE">min</span><span class="o" id="mwAWI">-</span><span class="n" id="mwAWM">priority</span><span class="o" id="mwAWQ">-</span><span class="n" id="mwAWU">queue</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAWY">  </span><span class="k" id="mwAWc">while</span><span class="w" id="mwAWg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAWk">S</span><span class="w" id="mwAWo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAWs">has</span><span class="w" id="mwAWw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAW0">data</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAW4">    </span><span class="n" id="mwAW8">r</span><span class="w" id="mwAXA"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAXE">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwAXI"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwAXM">random</span><span class="p" id="mwAXQ">()</span><span class="w" id="mwAXU">   </span><span class="c1" id="mwAXY">// uniformly random between 0 and 1, exclusive</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAXc">    </span><span class="k" id="mwAXg">if</span><span class="w" id="mwAXk"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAXo">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAXs">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAXw">Count</span><span class="w" id="mwAX0"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAX4">&lt;</span><span class="w" id="mwAX8"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAYA">k</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAYE">      </span><span class="n" id="mwAYI">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAYM">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAYQ">Insert</span><span class="p" id="mwAYU">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAYY">r</span><span class="o" id="mwAYc">,</span><span class="w" id="mwAYg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAYk">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAYo">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAYs">Current</span><span class="p" id="mwAYw">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAY0">    </span><span class="k" id="mwAY4">else</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAY8">      </span><span class="c1" id="mwAZA">// keep k items with largest associated keys</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAZE">      </span><span class="k" id="mwAZI">if</span><span class="w" id="mwAZM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAZQ">r</span><span class="w" id="mwAZU"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAZY">></span><span class="w" id="mwAZc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAZg">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAZk">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAZo">Minimum</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAZs">        </span><span class="n" id="mwAZw">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAZ0">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAZ4">Extract</span><span class="o" id="mwAZ8">-</span><span class="nb" id="mwAaA">Min</span><span class="p" id="mwAaE">()</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAaI">        </span><span class="n" id="mwAaM">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAaQ">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAaU">Insert</span><span class="p" id="mwAaY">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAac">r</span><span class="o" id="mwAag">,</span><span class="w" id="mwAak"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAao">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAas">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAaw">Current</span><span class="p" id="mwAa0">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAa4">    </span><span class="k" id="mwAa8">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAbA">    </span><span class="n" id="mwAbE">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAbI">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAbM">Next</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAbQ">  </span><span class="k" id="mwAbU">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAbY">  </span><span class="n" id="mwAbc">return</span><span class="w" id="mwAbg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAbk">items</span><span class="w" id="mwAbo"> </span><span class="k" id="mwAbs">in</span><span class="w" id="mwAbw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAb0">H</span>
<span class="k" id="mwAb4">end</span>
</pre></div>

<p id="mwAb8">The expected running time of this algorithm is <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt141" id="mwAcA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(n + k \\log k \\log(n/k))"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(n+k\log k\log(n/k))}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>k</mi>
        <mi>log</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>k</mi>
        <mi>log</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mo>/</mo>
        </mrow>
        <mi>k</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle O(n+k\log k\log(n/k))}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/f2514ee1850f891fc696162f655f5efee07a3bab" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:22.923ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle O(n+k\log k\log(n/k))}"/></span> and it is relevant mainly because it can easily be extended to items with weights.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="6" id="mwAcE" aria-labelledby="Weighted_random_sampling"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Weighted_random_sampling">Weighted random sampling</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Weighted random sampling"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAcI">The methods presented in the previous sections do not allow to obtain a priori fixed inclusion probabilities.
Some applications require items' sampling probabilities to be according to weights associated with each item. For example, it might be required to sample queries in a search engine with weight as number of times they were performed so that the sample can be analyzed for overall impact on user experience. Let the weight of item <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt142" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAcM" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"i"}},"i":0}}]}'>i</span> be <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt144" id="mwAcQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"w_i"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle w_{i}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>w</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>i</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle w_{i}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/fe22f0329d3ecb2e1880d44d191aba0e5475db68" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:2.464ex; height:2.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle w_{i}}"/></span>, and the sum of all weights be <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt143" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAcU" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"W"}},"i":0}}]}'>W</span>. There are two ways to interpret weights assigned to each item in the set:<sup about="#mwt147" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-efraimidis_12-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"efraimidis"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-efraimidis-12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-efraimidis-12" id="mwAcY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAcc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcg">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAck">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<ol id="mwAco"><li id="mwAcs">In each round, the probability of every <i id="mwAcw">unselected</i> item to be selected in that round is proportional to its weight relative to the weights of all unselected items.  If <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt148" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAc0" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"X"}},"i":0}}]}'>X</span> is the current sample, then the probability of an item <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt149" id="mwAc4" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"i \\notin X"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle i\notin X}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>i</mi>
        <mo>∉<!-- ∉ --></mo>
        <mi>X</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle i\notin X}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a4e88ed03aeae15b7638fd00acffeaf6302899ef" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:5.623ex; height:2.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle i\notin X}"/></span> to be selected in the current round is <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt150" id="mwAc8" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\textstyle w_i/(W - \\sum_{j \\in X}{w_j})"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \textstyle w_{i}/(W-\sum _{j\in X}{w_{j}})}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mstyle displaystyle="false" scriptlevel="0">
          <msub>
            <mi>w</mi>
            <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
              <mi>i</mi>
            </mrow>
          </msub>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mo>/</mo>
          </mrow>
          <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
          <mi>W</mi>
          <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
          <munder>
            <mo>∑<!-- ∑ --></mo>
            <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
              <mi>j</mi>
              <mo>∈<!-- ∈ --></mo>
              <mi>X</mi>
            </mrow>
          </munder>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <msub>
              <mi>w</mi>
              <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
                <mi>j</mi>
              </mrow>
            </msub>
          </mrow>
          <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        </mstyle>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle \textstyle w_{i}/(W-\sum _{j\in X}{w_{j}})}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/f5c86c58130dbee7d73d19379750ed9066a20d8e" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -1.338ex; width:19.532ex; height:3.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle \textstyle w_{i}/(W-\sum _{j\in X}{w_{j}})}"/></span>.</li>
<li id="mwAdA">The probability of each item to be included in the random sample is proportional to its relative weight, i.e., <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt151" id="mwAdE" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"w_i / W"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle w_{i}/W}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>w</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>i</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mo>/</mo>
        </mrow>
        <mi>W</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle w_{i}/W}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/486cd463082b32ce5a10de17419e10bc731a653a" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:6.062ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle w_{i}/W}"/></span>. It is not achievable when <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt152" id="mwAdI" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" k=n "}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k=n}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>k</mi>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k=n}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c4b8cc6fcba0c0b24656b0fb33414d2e6cffb83c" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:5.704ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k=n}"/></span>, since each item is then included with probability 1.</li></ol>

<section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwAdM" aria-labelledby="Algorithm_A-Res"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Algorithm_A-Res">Algorithm A-Res</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Algorithm A-Res"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div><p id="mwAdQ">
The following algorithm was given by Efraimidis and Spirakis that uses interpretation 1:<sup about="#mwt159" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-EfrSpi_13-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"EfrSpi"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-EfrSpi-13"}}'><a href="#cite_note-EfrSpi-13" id="mwAdU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAdY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAdc">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAdg">]</span></span></a></sup></p><div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-pascal mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt160" id="mwAdk" data-mw='{"name":"syntaxhighlight","attrs":{"lang":"pascal"},"body":{"extsrc":"\n(*\n  S is a stream of items to sample\n  S.Current returns current item in stream\n  S.Weight  returns weight of current item in stream\n  S.Next advances stream to next position\n  The power operator is represented by ^\n  min-priority-queue supports:\n    Count -> number of items in priority queue\n    Minimum() -> returns minimum key value of all items\n    Extract-Min() -> Remove the item with minimum key\n    Insert(key, Item) -> Adds item with specified key\n *)\nReservoirSample(S[1..?])\n  H := new min-priority-queue\n  while S has data\n    r := random() ^ (1/S.Weight)   // random() produces a uniformly random number in (0,1)\n    if H.Count &lt; k\n      H.Insert(r, S.Current)\n    else\n      // keep k items with largest associated keys\n      if r > H.Minimum\n        H.Extract-Min()\n        H.Insert(r, S.Current)\n      end\n    end\n    S.Next\n  end\n  return items in H\nend\n"}}'><pre id="mwAdo"><span id="mwAds"></span><span class="cm" id="mwAdw">(*</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAd0">  S is a stream of items to sample</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAd4">  S.Current returns current item in stream</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAd8">  S.Weight  returns weight of current item in stream</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAeA">  S.Next advances stream to next position</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAeE">  The power operator is represented by ^</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAeI">  min-priority-queue supports:</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAeM">    Count -> number of items in priority queue</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAeQ">    Minimum() -> returns minimum key value of all items</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAeU">    Extract-Min() -> Remove the item with minimum key</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAeY">    Insert(key, Item) -> Adds item with specified key</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAec"> *)</span>
<span class="n" id="mwAeg">ReservoirSample</span><span class="p" id="mwAek">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAeo">S</span><span class="p" id="mwAes">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwAew">1</span><span class="o" id="mwAe0">..</span><span class="err" id="mwAe4">?</span><span class="p" id="mwAe8">])</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAfA">  </span><span class="n" id="mwAfE">H</span><span class="w" id="mwAfI"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAfM">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwAfQ"> </span><span class="k" id="mwAfU">new</span><span class="w" id="mwAfY"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwAfc">min</span><span class="o" id="mwAfg">-</span><span class="n" id="mwAfk">priority</span><span class="o" id="mwAfo">-</span><span class="n" id="mwAfs">queue</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAfw">  </span><span class="k" id="mwAf0">while</span><span class="w" id="mwAf4"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAf8">S</span><span class="w" id="mwAgA"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAgE">has</span><span class="w" id="mwAgI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAgM">data</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAgQ">    </span><span class="n" id="mwAgU">r</span><span class="w" id="mwAgY"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAgc">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwAgg"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwAgk">random</span><span class="p" id="mwAgo">()</span><span class="w" id="mwAgs"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAgw">^</span><span class="w" id="mwAg0"> </span><span class="p" id="mwAg4">(</span><span class="mi" id="mwAg8">1</span><span class="o" id="mwAhA">/</span><span class="n" id="mwAhE">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAhI">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAhM">Weight</span><span class="p" id="mwAhQ">)</span><span class="w" id="mwAhU">   </span><span class="c1" id="mwAhY">// random() produces a uniformly random number in (0,1)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAhc">    </span><span class="k" id="mwAhg">if</span><span class="w" id="mwAhk"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAho">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAhs">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAhw">Count</span><span class="w" id="mwAh0"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAh4">&lt;</span><span class="w" id="mwAh8"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAiA">k</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAiE">      </span><span class="n" id="mwAiI">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAiM">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAiQ">Insert</span><span class="p" id="mwAiU">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAiY">r</span><span class="o" id="mwAic">,</span><span class="w" id="mwAig"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAik">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAio">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAis">Current</span><span class="p" id="mwAiw">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAi0">    </span><span class="k" id="mwAi4">else</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAi8">      </span><span class="c1" id="mwAjA">// keep k items with largest associated keys</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAjE">      </span><span class="k" id="mwAjI">if</span><span class="w" id="mwAjM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAjQ">r</span><span class="w" id="mwAjU"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAjY">></span><span class="w" id="mwAjc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAjg">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAjk">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAjo">Minimum</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAjs">        </span><span class="n" id="mwAjw">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAj0">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAj4">Extract</span><span class="o" id="mwAj8">-</span><span class="nb" id="mwAkA">Min</span><span class="p" id="mwAkE">()</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAkI">        </span><span class="n" id="mwAkM">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAkQ">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAkU">Insert</span><span class="p" id="mwAkY">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAkc">r</span><span class="o" id="mwAkg">,</span><span class="w" id="mwAkk"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAko">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAks">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAkw">Current</span><span class="p" id="mwAk0">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAk4">      </span><span class="k" id="mwAk8">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAlA">    </span><span class="k" id="mwAlE">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAlI">    </span><span class="n" id="mwAlM">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAlQ">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAlU">Next</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAlY">  </span><span class="k" id="mwAlc">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAlg">  </span><span class="n" id="mwAlk">return</span><span class="w" id="mwAlo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAls">items</span><span class="w" id="mwAlw"> </span><span class="k" id="mwAl0">in</span><span class="w" id="mwAl4"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAl8">H</span>
<span class="k" id="mwAmA">end</span>
</pre></div><p id="mwAmE">This algorithm is identical to the algorithm given in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="#With_random_sort" class="mw-selflink-fragment" id="mwAmI">Reservoir Sampling with Random Sort</a> except for the generation of the items' keys. The algorithm is equivalent to assigning each item a key <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt161" id="mwAmM" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"r^{1/w_i}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle r^{1/w_{i}}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msup>
          <mi>r</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
              <mo>/</mo>
            </mrow>
            <msub>
              <mi>w</mi>
              <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
                <mi>i</mi>
              </mrow>
            </msub>
          </mrow>
        </msup>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle r^{1/w_{i}}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/df9d03514a4f58863221ba52eb5870054fcec3ae" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:4.726ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle r^{1/w_{i}}}"/></span> where <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt153" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAmQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"r"}},"i":0}}]}'>r</span> is the random number and then selecting the <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt154" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAmU" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"k"}},"i":0}}]}'>k</span> items with the largest keys.  Equivalently, a more <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Numerical_stability" title="Numerical stability" id="mwAmY">numerically stable</a> formulation of this algorithm computes the keys as <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt162" id="mwAmc" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"-\\ln(r)/w_i"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle -\ln(r)/w_{i}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
        <mi>ln</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>r</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mo>/</mo>
        </mrow>
        <msub>
          <mi>w</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>i</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle -\ln(r)/w_{i}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/3f9e09c18831f63083819261e4aa8fec396ddc67" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:10.619ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle -\ln(r)/w_{i}}"/></span> and select the <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt155" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAmg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"k"}},"i":0}}]}'>k</span> items with the <i id="mwAmk">smallest</i> keys.<sup about="#mwt166" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-14" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-14" id="mwAmo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAms"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmw">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAm0">]</span></span></a></sup><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_failed_verification" about="#mwt156" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAm4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Not in source","href":"./Template:Not_in_source"},"params":{"reason":{"wt":"The statement is correct, I think; however the source cited mentions the exponential distribution (Sect.1.3, p.6) but doesn&apos;t seem to mention any relationship with the Efraimidis and Spirakis algorithm, or anything about stability."},"date":{"wt":"December 2020"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_failed_verification_from_December_2020" about="#mwt156"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template " style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt156" id="mwAm8"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability"><span title="The statement is correct, I think; however the source cited mentions the exponential distribution (Sect.1.3, p.6) but doesn't seem to mention any relationship with the Efraimidis and Spirakis algorithm, or anything about stability. </section><section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwAnA" aria-labelledby="Algorithm_A-ExpJ"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Algorithm_A-ExpJ">Algorithm A-ExpJ</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Algorithm A-ExpJ"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAnE">The following algorithm is a more efficient version of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="#Algorithm_A-Res" class="mw-selflink-fragment" id="mwAnI">A-Res</a>, also given by Efraimidis and Spirakis:<sup about="#mwt167" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-EfrSpi_13-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"EfrSpi"}}'><a href="#cite_note-EfrSpi-13" id="mwAnM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAnQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnU">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnY">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-pascal mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt168" id="mwAnc" data-mw='{"name":"syntaxhighlight","attrs":{"lang":"pascal"},"body":{"extsrc":"\n(*\n  S is a stream of items to sample\n  S.Current returns current item in stream\n  S.Weight  returns weight of current item in stream\n  S.Next advances stream to next position\n  The power operator is represented by ^\n  min-priority-queue supports:\n    Count -> number of items in the priority queue\n    Minimum -> minimum key of any item in the priority queue\n    Extract-Min() -> Remove the item with minimum key\n    Insert(Key, Item) -> Adds item with specified key\n *)\nReservoirSampleWithJumps(S[1..?])\n  H := new min-priority-queue\n  while S has data and H.Count &lt; k\n    r := random() ^ (1/S.Weight)   // random() produces a uniformly random number in (0,1)\n    H.Insert(r, S.Current)\n    S.Next\n  end\n  X := log(random()) / log(H.Minimum) // this is the amount of weight that needs to be jumped over\n  while S has data\n    X := X - S.Weight\n    if X &lt;= 0\n      t := H.Minimum ^ S.Weight\n      r := random(t, 1) ^ (1/S.Weight) // random(x, y) produces a uniformly random number in (x, y)\n    \n      H.Extract-Min()\n      H.Insert(r, S.Current)\n\n      X := log(random()) / log(H.Minimum)\n    end\n    S.Next\n  end\n  return items in H\nend\n"}}'><pre id="mwAng"><span id="mwAnk"></span><span class="cm" id="mwAno">(*</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAns">  S is a stream of items to sample</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAnw">  S.Current returns current item in stream</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAn0">  S.Weight  returns weight of current item in stream</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAn4">  S.Next advances stream to next position</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAn8">  The power operator is represented by ^</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAoA">  min-priority-queue supports:</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAoE">    Count -> number of items in the priority queue</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAoI">    Minimum -> minimum key of any item in the priority queue</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAoM">    Extract-Min() -> Remove the item with minimum key</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAoQ">    Insert(Key, Item) -> Adds item with specified key</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwAoU"> *)</span>
<span class="n" id="mwAoY">ReservoirSampleWithJumps</span><span class="p" id="mwAoc">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAog">S</span><span class="p" id="mwAok">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwAoo">1</span><span class="o" id="mwAos">..</span><span class="err" id="mwAow">?</span><span class="p" id="mwAo0">])</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAo4">  </span><span class="n" id="mwAo8">H</span><span class="w" id="mwApA"> </span><span class="o" id="mwApE">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwApI"> </span><span class="k" id="mwApM">new</span><span class="w" id="mwApQ"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwApU">min</span><span class="o" id="mwApY">-</span><span class="n" id="mwApc">priority</span><span class="o" id="mwApg">-</span><span class="n" id="mwApk">queue</span>
<span class="w" id="mwApo">  </span><span class="k" id="mwAps">while</span><span class="w" id="mwApw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAp0">S</span><span class="w" id="mwAp4"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAp8">has</span><span class="w" id="mwAqA"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAqE">data</span><span class="w" id="mwAqI"> </span><span class="k" id="mwAqM">and</span><span class="w" id="mwAqQ"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAqU">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAqY">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAqc">Count</span><span class="w" id="mwAqg"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAqk">&lt;</span><span class="w" id="mwAqo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAqs">k</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAqw">    </span><span class="n" id="mwAq0">r</span><span class="w" id="mwAq4"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAq8">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwArA"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwArE">random</span><span class="p" id="mwArI">()</span><span class="w" id="mwArM"> </span><span class="o" id="mwArQ">^</span><span class="w" id="mwArU"> </span><span class="p" id="mwArY">(</span><span class="mi" id="mwArc">1</span><span class="o" id="mwArg">/</span><span class="n" id="mwArk">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAro">.</span><span class="n" id="mwArs">Weight</span><span class="p" id="mwArw">)</span><span class="w" id="mwAr0">   </span><span class="c1" id="mwAr4">// random() produces a uniformly random number in (0,1)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAr8">    </span><span class="n" id="mwAsA">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAsE">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAsI">Insert</span><span class="p" id="mwAsM">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAsQ">r</span><span class="o" id="mwAsU">,</span><span class="w" id="mwAsY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAsc">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAsg">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAsk">Current</span><span class="p" id="mwAso">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAss">    </span><span class="n" id="mwAsw">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAs0">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAs4">Next</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAs8">  </span><span class="k" id="mwAtA">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAtE">  </span><span class="n" id="mwAtI">X</span><span class="w" id="mwAtM"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAtQ">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwAtU"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAtY">log</span><span class="p" id="mwAtc">(</span><span class="nb" id="mwAtg">random</span><span class="p" id="mwAtk">())</span><span class="w" id="mwAto"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAts">/</span><span class="w" id="mwAtw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAt0">log</span><span class="p" id="mwAt4">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAt8">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAuA">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAuE">Minimum</span><span class="p" id="mwAuI">)</span><span class="w" id="mwAuM"> </span><span class="c1" id="mwAuQ">// this is the amount of weight that needs to be jumped over</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAuU">  </span><span class="k" id="mwAuY">while</span><span class="w" id="mwAuc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAug">S</span><span class="w" id="mwAuk"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAuo">has</span><span class="w" id="mwAus"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAuw">data</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAu0">    </span><span class="n" id="mwAu4">X</span><span class="w" id="mwAu8"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAvA">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwAvE"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAvI">X</span><span class="w" id="mwAvM"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAvQ">-</span><span class="w" id="mwAvU"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAvY">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAvc">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAvg">Weight</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAvk">    </span><span class="k" id="mwAvo">if</span><span class="w" id="mwAvs"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAvw">X</span><span class="w" id="mwAv0"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAv4">&lt;=</span><span class="w" id="mwAv8"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwAwA">0</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAwE">      </span><span class="n" id="mwAwI">t</span><span class="w" id="mwAwM"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAwQ">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwAwU"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAwY">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAwc">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAwg">Minimum</span><span class="w" id="mwAwk"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAwo">^</span><span class="w" id="mwAws"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAww">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAw0">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAw4">Weight</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAw8">      </span><span class="n" id="mwAxA">r</span><span class="w" id="mwAxE"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAxI">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwAxM"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwAxQ">random</span><span class="p" id="mwAxU">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAxY">t</span><span class="o" id="mwAxc">,</span><span class="w" id="mwAxg"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwAxk">1</span><span class="p" id="mwAxo">)</span><span class="w" id="mwAxs"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAxw">^</span><span class="w" id="mwAx0"> </span><span class="p" id="mwAx4">(</span><span class="mi" id="mwAx8">1</span><span class="o" id="mwAyA">/</span><span class="n" id="mwAyE">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAyI">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAyM">Weight</span><span class="p" id="mwAyQ">)</span><span class="w" id="mwAyU"> </span><span class="c1" id="mwAyY">// random(x, y) produces a uniformly random number in (x, y)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAyc">    </span>
<span class="w" id="mwAyg">      </span><span class="n" id="mwAyk">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAyo">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAys">Extract</span><span class="o" id="mwAyw">-</span><span class="nb" id="mwAy0">Min</span><span class="p" id="mwAy4">()</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAy8">      </span><span class="n" id="mwAzA">H</span><span class="o" id="mwAzE">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAzI">Insert</span><span class="p" id="mwAzM">(</span><span class="n" id="mwAzQ">r</span><span class="o" id="mwAzU">,</span><span class="w" id="mwAzY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwAzc">S</span><span class="o" id="mwAzg">.</span><span class="n" id="mwAzk">Current</span><span class="p" id="mwAzo">)</span>

<span class="w" id="mwAzs">      </span><span class="n" id="mwAzw">X</span><span class="w" id="mwAz0"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAz4">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwAz8"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA0A">log</span><span class="p" id="mwA0E">(</span><span class="nb" id="mwA0I">random</span><span class="p" id="mwA0M">())</span><span class="w" id="mwA0Q"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA0U">/</span><span class="w" id="mwA0Y"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA0c">log</span><span class="p" id="mwA0g">(</span><span class="n" id="mwA0k">H</span><span class="o" id="mwA0o">.</span><span class="n" id="mwA0s">Minimum</span><span class="p" id="mwA0w">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA00">    </span><span class="k" id="mwA04">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA08">    </span><span class="n" id="mwA1A">S</span><span class="o" id="mwA1E">.</span><span class="n" id="mwA1I">Next</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA1M">  </span><span class="k" id="mwA1Q">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA1U">  </span><span class="n" id="mwA1Y">return</span><span class="w" id="mwA1c"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA1g">items</span><span class="w" id="mwA1k"> </span><span class="k" id="mwA1o">in</span><span class="w" id="mwA1s"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA1w">H</span>
<span class="k" id="mwA10">end</span>
</pre></div>

<p id="mwA14">This algorithm follows the same mathematical properties that are used in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="#Algorithm_A-Res" class="mw-selflink-fragment" id="mwA18">A-Res</a>, but instead of calculating the key for each item and checking whether that item should be inserted or not, it calculates an exponential jump to the next item which will be inserted. This avoids having to create random variates for each item, which may be expensive. The number of random variates required is reduced from <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt169" id="mwA2A" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(n)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle O(n)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/34109fe397fdcff370079185bfdb65826cb5565a" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.977ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle O(n)}"/></span> to <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt170" id="mwA2E" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(k \\log (n/k))"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(k\log(n/k))}">
  <semantics>

      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">

      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
