    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c3c9a2c7b599b37105512c5d570edc034056dd40" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.211ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k}"/></span> is the reservoir size, and <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt172" id="mwA2M" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>n</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a601995d55609f2d9f5e233e36fbe9ea26011b3b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.395ex; height:1.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n}"/></span> is the number of items in the stream.<sup about="#mwt173" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-EfrSpi_13-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"EfrSpi"}}'><a href="#cite_note-EfrSpi-13" id="mwA2Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA2U"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2Y">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2c">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwA2g" aria-labelledby="Algorithm_A-Chao"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Algorithm_A-Chao">Algorithm A-Chao</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Algorithm A-Chao"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA2k">Warning: the following description is wrong, see Chao's original paper and the discussion <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://stackoverflow.com/questions/58310136/initialization-of-weighted-reservoir-sampling-a-chao-implementation" class="external text" id="mwA2o">here</a>.</p>
<p id="mwA2s">Following algorithm was given by M. T. Chao uses interpretation 2:<sup about="#mwt176" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:1_15-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":1"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:1-15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:1-15" id="mwA2w"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA20"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA24">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA28">]</span></span></a></sup> and Tillé (2006).<sup about="#mwt179" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-16" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-16" id="mwA3A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA3E"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3I">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3M">]</span></span></a></sup></p><div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-pascal mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt180" id="mwA3Q" data-mw='{"name":"syntaxhighlight","attrs":{"lang":"pascal"},"body":{"extsrc":"\n(*\n  S has items to sample, R will contain the result\n  S[i].Weight contains weight for each item\n *)\nWeightedReservoir-Chao(S[1..n], R[1..k])\n  WSum := 0\n  // fill the reservoir array\n  for i := 1 to k\n      R[i] := S[i]\n      WSum := WSum + S[i].Weight\n  end\n  for i := k+1 to n\n    WSum := WSum + S[i].Weight\n    p := S[i].Weight / WSum // probability for this item\n    j := random();          // uniformly random between 0 and 1\n    if j &lt;= p               // select item according to probability\n        R[randomInteger(1,k)] := S[i]  //uniform selection in reservoir for replacement\n    end\n  end\nend\n"}}'><pre id="mwA3U"><span id="mwA3Y"></span><span class="cm" id="mwA3c">(*</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwA3g">  S has items to sample, R will contain the result</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwA3k">  S[i].Weight contains weight for each item</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwA3o"> *)</span>
<span class="n" id="mwA3s">WeightedReservoir</span><span class="o" id="mwA3w">-</span><span class="n" id="mwA30">Chao</span><span class="p" id="mwA34">(</span><span class="n" id="mwA38">S</span><span class="p" id="mwA4A">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwA4E">1</span><span class="o" id="mwA4I">..</span><span class="n" id="mwA4M">n</span><span class="p" id="mwA4Q">]</span><span class="o" id="mwA4U">,</span><span class="w" id="mwA4Y"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA4c">R</span><span class="p" id="mwA4g">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwA4k">1</span><span class="o" id="mwA4o">..</span><span class="n" id="mwA4s">k</span><span class="p" id="mwA4w">])</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA40">  </span><span class="n" id="mwA44">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwA48"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA5A">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwA5E"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwA5I">0</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA5M">  </span><span class="c1" id="mwA5Q">// fill the reservoir array</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA5U">  </span><span class="k" id="mwA5Y">for</span><span class="w" id="mwA5c"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA5g">i</span><span class="w" id="mwA5k"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA5o">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwA5s"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwA5w">1</span><span class="w" id="mwA50"> </span><span class="k" id="mwA54">to</span><span class="w" id="mwA58"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA6A">k</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA6E">      </span><span class="n" id="mwA6I">R</span><span class="p" id="mwA6M">[</span><span class="n" id="mwA6Q">i</span><span class="p" id="mwA6U">]</span><span class="w" id="mwA6Y"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA6c">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwA6g"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA6k">S</span><span class="p" id="mwA6o">[</span><span class="n" id="mwA6s">i</span><span class="p" id="mwA6w">]</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA60">      </span><span class="n" id="mwA64">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwA68"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA7A">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwA7E"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA7I">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwA7M"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA7Q">+</span><span class="w" id="mwA7U"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA7Y">S</span><span class="p" id="mwA7c">[</span><span class="n" id="mwA7g">i</span><span class="p" id="mwA7k">]</span><span class="o" id="mwA7o">.</span><span class="n" id="mwA7s">Weight</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA7w">  </span><span class="k" id="mwA70">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA74">  </span><span class="k" id="mwA78">for</span><span class="w" id="mwA8A"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA8E">i</span><span class="w" id="mwA8I"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA8M">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwA8Q"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA8U">k</span><span class="o" id="mwA8Y">+</span><span class="mi" id="mwA8c">1</span><span class="w" id="mwA8g"> </span><span class="k" id="mwA8k">to</span><span class="w" id="mwA8o"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA8s">n</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA8w">    </span><span class="n" id="mwA80">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwA84"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA88">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwA9A"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA9E">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwA9I"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA9M">+</span><span class="w" id="mwA9Q"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA9U">S</span><span class="p" id="mwA9Y">[</span><span class="n" id="mwA9c">i</span><span class="p" id="mwA9g">]</span><span class="o" id="mwA9k">.</span><span class="n" id="mwA9o">Weight</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA9s">    </span><span class="n" id="mwA9w">p</span><span class="w" id="mwA90"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA94">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwA98"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA-A">S</span><span class="p" id="mwA-E">[</span><span class="n" id="mwA-I">i</span><span class="p" id="mwA-M">]</span><span class="o" id="mwA-Q">.</span><span class="n" id="mwA-U">Weight</span><span class="w" id="mwA-Y"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA-c">/</span><span class="w" id="mwA-g"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA-k">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwA-o"> </span><span class="c1" id="mwA-s">// probability for this item</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA-w">    </span><span class="n" id="mwA-0">j</span><span class="w" id="mwA-4"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA-8">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwA_A"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwA_E">random</span><span class="p" id="mwA_I">()</span><span class="o" id="mwA_M">;</span><span class="w" id="mwA_Q">          </span><span class="c1" id="mwA_U">// uniformly random between 0 and 1</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA_Y">    </span><span class="k" id="mwA_c">if</span><span class="w" id="mwA_g"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA_k">j</span><span class="w" id="mwA_o"> </span><span class="o" id="mwA_s">&lt;=</span><span class="w" id="mwA_w"> </span><span class="n" id="mwA_0">p</span><span class="w" id="mwA_4">               </span><span class="c1" id="mwA_8">// select item according to probability</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBAA">        </span><span class="n" id="mwBAE">R</span><span class="p" id="mwBAI">[</span><span class="n" id="mwBAM">randomInteger</span><span class="p" id="mwBAQ">(</span><span class="mi" id="mwBAU">1</span><span class="o" id="mwBAY">,</span><span class="n" id="mwBAc">k</span><span class="p" id="mwBAg">)]</span><span class="w" id="mwBAk"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBAo">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBAs"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBAw">S</span><span class="p" id="mwBA0">[</span><span class="n" id="mwBA4">i</span><span class="p" id="mwBA8">]</span><span class="w" id="mwBBA">  </span><span class="c1" id="mwBBE">//uniform selection in reservoir for replacement</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBBI">    </span><span class="k" id="mwBBM">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBBQ">  </span><span class="k" id="mwBBU">end</span>
<span class="k" id="mwBBY">end</span>
</pre></div><p id="mwBBc">For each item, its relative weight is calculated and used to randomly decide if the item will be added into the reservoir. If the item is selected, then one of the existing items of the reservoir is uniformly selected and replaced with the new item. The trick here is that, if the probabilities of all items in the reservoir are already proportional to their weights, then by selecting uniformly which item to replace, the probabilities of all items remain proportional to their weight after the replacement.</p>

<p id="mwBBg">Note that Chao doesn't specify how to sample the first <i id="mwBBk">k</i> elements.
He simple assumes we have some other way of picking them in proportion to their weight.
Chao: "Assume that we have a sampling plan of fixed size with respect to <i id="mwBBo">S_k</i> at time <i id="mwBBs">A</i>; such that its first-order inclusion probability of <i id="mwBBw">X_t</i> is <i id="mwBB0">π(k; i)</i>".</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="10" id="mwBB4" aria-labelledby="Algorithm_A-Chao_with_Jumps"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Algorithm_A-Chao_with_Jumps">Algorithm A-Chao with Jumps</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Algorithm A-Chao with Jumps"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBB8">Similar to the other algorithms, it is possible to compute a random weight <code id="mwBCA">j</code> and subtract items' probability mass values, skipping them while <code id="mwBCE">j > 0</code>, reducing the number of random numbers that have to be generated.<sup about="#mwt181" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-efraimidis_12-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"efraimidis"}}'><a href="#cite_note-efraimidis-12" id="mwBCI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBCM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBCQ">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBCU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-pascal mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt182" id="mwBCY" data-mw='{"name":"syntaxhighlight","attrs":{"lang":"pascal"},"body":{"extsrc":"\n(*\n  S has items to sample, R will contain the result\n  S[i].Weight contains weight for each item\n *)\nWeightedReservoir-Chao(S[1..n], R[1..k])\n  WSum := 0\n  // fill the reservoir array\n  for i := 1 to k\n      R[i] := S[i]\n      WSum := WSum + S[i].Weight\n  end\n  j := random() // uniformly random between 0 and 1\n  pNone := 1 // probability that no item has been selected so far (in this jump)\n  for i := k+1 to n\n    WSum := WSum + S[i].Weight\n    p := S[i].Weight / WSum // probability for this item\n    j -= p * pNone\n    pNone := pNone * (1 - p)\n    if j &lt;= 0\n        R[randomInteger(1,k)] := S[i]  //uniform selection in reservoir for replacement\n        j = random()\n        pNone := 1\n    end\n  end\nend\n"}}'><pre id="mwBCc"><span id="mwBCg"></span><span class="cm" id="mwBCk">(*</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwBCo">  S has items to sample, R will contain the result</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwBCs">  S[i].Weight contains weight for each item</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwBCw"> *)</span>
<span class="n" id="mwBC0">WeightedReservoir</span><span class="o" id="mwBC4">-</span><span class="n" id="mwBC8">Chao</span><span class="p" id="mwBDA">(</span><span class="n" id="mwBDE">S</span><span class="p" id="mwBDI">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwBDM">1</span><span class="o" id="mwBDQ">..</span><span class="n" id="mwBDU">n</span><span class="p" id="mwBDY">]</span><span class="o" id="mwBDc">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBDg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBDk">R</span><span class="p" id="mwBDo">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwBDs">1</span><span class="o" id="mwBDw">..</span><span class="n" id="mwBD0">k</span><span class="p" id="mwBD4">])</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBD8">  </span><span class="n" id="mwBEA">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwBEE"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBEI">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBEM"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwBEQ">0</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBEU">  </span><span class="c1" id="mwBEY">// fill the reservoir array</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBEc">  </span><span class="k" id="mwBEg">for</span><span class="w" id="mwBEk"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBEo">i</span><span class="w" id="mwBEs"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBEw">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBE0"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwBE4">1</span><span class="w" id="mwBE8"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBFA">to</span><span class="w" id="mwBFE"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBFI">k</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBFM">      </span><span class="n" id="mwBFQ">R</span><span class="p" id="mwBFU">[</span><span class="n" id="mwBFY">i</span><span class="p" id="mwBFc">]</span><span class="w" id="mwBFg"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBFk">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBFo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBFs">S</span><span class="p" id="mwBFw">[</span><span class="n" id="mwBF0">i</span><span class="p" id="mwBF4">]</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBF8">      </span><span class="n" id="mwBGA">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwBGE"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBGI">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBGM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBGQ">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwBGU"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBGY">+</span><span class="w" id="mwBGc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBGg">S</span><span class="p" id="mwBGk">[</span><span class="n" id="mwBGo">i</span><span class="p" id="mwBGs">]</span><span class="o" id="mwBGw">.</span><span class="n" id="mwBG0">Weight</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBG4">  </span><span class="k" id="mwBG8">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBHA">  </span><span class="n" id="mwBHE">j</span><span class="w" id="mwBHI"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBHM">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBHQ"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwBHU">random</span><span class="p" id="mwBHY">()</span><span class="w" id="mwBHc"> </span><span class="c1" id="mwBHg">// uniformly random between 0 and 1</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBHk">  </span><span class="n" id="mwBHo">pNone</span><span class="w" id="mwBHs"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBHw">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBH0"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwBH4">1</span><span class="w" id="mwBH8"> </span><span class="c1" id="mwBIA">// probability that no item has been selected so far (in this jump)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBIE">  </span><span class="k" id="mwBII">for</span><span class="w" id="mwBIM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBIQ">i</span><span class="w" id="mwBIU"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBIY">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBIc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBIg">k</span><span class="o" id="mwBIk">+</span><span class="mi" id="mwBIo">1</span><span class="w" id="mwBIs"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBIw">to</span><span class="w" id="mwBI0"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBI4">n</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBI8">    </span><span class="n" id="mwBJA">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwBJE"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBJI">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBJM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBJQ">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwBJU"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBJY">+</span><span class="w" id="mwBJc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBJg">S</span><span class="p" id="mwBJk">[</span><span class="n" id="mwBJo">i</span><span class="p" id="mwBJs">]</span><span class="o" id="mwBJw">.</span><span class="n" id="mwBJ0">Weight</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBJ4">    </span><span class="n" id="mwBJ8">p</span><span class="w" id="mwBKA"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBKE">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBKI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBKM">S</span><span class="p" id="mwBKQ">[</span><span class="n" id="mwBKU">i</span><span class="p" id="mwBKY">]</span><span class="o" id="mwBKc">.</span><span class="n" id="mwBKg">Weight</span><span class="w" id="mwBKk"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBKo">/</span><span class="w" id="mwBKs"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBKw">WSum</span><span class="w" id="mwBK0"> </span><span class="c1" id="mwBK4">// probability for this item</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBK8">    </span><span class="n" id="mwBLA">j</span><span class="w" id="mwBLE"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBLI">-=</span><span class="w" id="mwBLM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBLQ">p</span><span class="w" id="mwBLU"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBLY">*</span><span class="w" id="mwBLc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBLg">pNone</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBLk">    </span><span class="n" id="mwBLo">pNone</span><span class="w" id="mwBLs"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBLw">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBL0"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBL4">pNone</span><span class="w" id="mwBL8"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBMA">*</span><span class="w" id="mwBME"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBMI">(</span><span class="mi" id="mwBMM">1</span><span class="w" id="mwBMQ"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBMU">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBMY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBMc">p</span><span class="p" id="mwBMg">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBMk">    </span><span class="k" id="mwBMo">if</span><span class="w" id="mwBMs"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBMw">j</span><span class="w" id="mwBM0"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBM4">&lt;=</span><span class="w" id="mwBM8"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwBNA">0</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBNE">        </span><span class="n" id="mwBNI">R</span><span class="p" id="mwBNM">[</span><span class="n" id="mwBNQ">randomInteger</span><span class="p" id="mwBNU">(</span><span class="mi" id="mwBNY">1</span><span class="o" id="mwBNc">,</span><span class="n" id="mwBNg">k</span><span class="p" id="mwBNk">)]</span><span class="w" id="mwBNo"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBNs">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBNw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBN0">S</span><span class="p" id="mwBN4">[</span><span class="n" id="mwBN8">i</span><span class="p" id="mwBOA">]</span><span class="w" id="mwBOE">  </span><span class="c1" id="mwBOI">//uniform selection in reservoir for replacement</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBOM">        </span><span class="n" id="mwBOQ">j</span><span class="w" id="mwBOU"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBOY">=</span><span class="w" id="mwBOc"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwBOg">random</span><span class="p" id="mwBOk">()</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBOo">        </span><span class="n" id="mwBOs">pNone</span><span class="w" id="mwBOw"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBO0">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBO4"> </span><span class="mi" id="mwBO8">1</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBPA">    </span><span class="k" id="mwBPE">end</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBPI">  </span><span class="k" id="mwBPM">end</span>
<span class="k" id="mwBPQ">end</span>
</pre></div>

</section></section></section><section data-mw-section-id="11" id="mwBPU" aria-labelledby="Applications_for_Multi-Class_Fairness"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Applications_for_Multi-Class_Fairness">Applications for Multi-Class Fairness</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Applications for Multi-Class Fairness"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwBPY">In <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Machine_learning" title="Machine learning" id="mwBPc">machine learning</a> applications, fairness is a critical consideration, especially in scenarios where data streams exhibit class imbalance. To address this, Nikoloutsopoulos, Titsias, and Koutsopoulos proposed the Kullback-Leibler Reservoir Sampling (KLRS) algorithm as a solution to the challenges of Continual Learning, where models must learn incrementally from a continuous data stream.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="12" id="mwBPg" aria-labelledby="KLRS_Algorithm"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="KLRS_Algorithm">KLRS Algorithm</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section: KLRS Algorithm"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBPk">The KLRS algorithm was designed to create a flexible policy that matches class percentages in the buffer to a target distribution while employing Reservoir Sampling techniques. This is achieved by minimizing the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence between the current buffer distribution and the desired target distribution.</p>

<p id="mwBPo">KLRS generalizes earlier methods like Reservoir Sampling and Class-Balancing Reservoir Sampling, as verified by experiments using confidence intervals, demonstrating its broader applicability and improved performance.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="13" id="mwBPs" aria-labelledby="Algorithm_Description"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Algorithm_Description">Algorithm Description</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: Algorithm Description"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBPw">The KLRS algorithm operates by maintaining a buffer of size  and updating its contents as new data points arrive in a stream. Below is the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pseudocode" title="Pseudocode" id="mwBP0">pseudocode</a> for the KLRS algorithm:</p>

</section></section></section><section data-mw-section-id="14" id="mwBP4" aria-labelledby="Algorithm:_KLRS"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Algorithm:_KLRS">Algorithm: KLRS</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Algorithm: KLRS"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-pascal mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt183" id="mwBP8" data-mw='{"name":"syntaxhighlight","attrs":{"lang":"pascal"},"body":{"extsrc":"\nKLRS(Stream, BufferSize M, TargetDistribution)\n  Input:\n    * Stream (data points (x, y) arriving sequentially)\n    * BufferSize M\n    * TargetDistribution (desired class distribution in the buffer)\n\n  Output: \n    Updated buffer maintaining the target distribution\n\n  Initialize buffer M with the first M data points from the stream.\n  \n  For t = M+1 to ∞:\n    - Update stream counters to compute n_{t+1}.\n    - Compute target distribution p_{t+1}.\n    - For each class k in the buffer:\n      - Simulate removing one instance of class k and adding (x_t, y_t).\n      - Compute KL divergence v_k between p_{t+1} and the modified buffer distribution.\n    - Select the class k* with the minimum v_k.\n    - If k* = y_t:\n      - Generate random number r in [1, n_{t+1}, y_t].\n      - If r ≤ m_{t, y_t},\n        - replace a random instance of class y_t in the buffer with (x_t, y_t).\n    - Else, replace a random instance of the selected class k* in the buffer with (x_t, y_t).\n    - Update buffer counters m_{t+1].\n    - Update class set C_{t+1} := unique(C_t ∪ {y_t}).\n"}}'><pre id="mwBQA"><span id="mwBQE"></span><span class="n" id="mwBQI">KLRS</span><span class="p" id="mwBQM">(</span><span class="n" id="mwBQQ">Stream</span><span class="o" id="mwBQU">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBQY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBQc">BufferSize</span><span class="w" id="mwBQg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBQk">M</span><span class="o" id="mwBQo">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBQs"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBQw">TargetDistribution</span><span class="p" id="mwBQ0">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBQ4">  </span><span class="n" id="mwBQ8">Input</span><span class="o" id="mwBRA">:</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBRE">    </span><span class="o" id="mwBRI">*</span><span class="w" id="mwBRM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBRQ">Stream</span><span class="w" id="mwBRU"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBRY">(</span><span class="n" id="mwBRc">data</span><span class="w" id="mwBRg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBRk">points</span><span class="w" id="mwBRo"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBRs">(</span><span class="n" id="mwBRw">x</span><span class="o" id="mwBR0">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBR4"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBR8">y</span><span class="p" id="mwBSA">)</span><span class="w" id="mwBSE"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBSI">arriving</span><span class="w" id="mwBSM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBSQ">sequentially</span><span class="p" id="mwBSU">)</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBSY">    </span><span class="o" id="mwBSc">*</span><span class="w" id="mwBSg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBSk">BufferSize</span><span class="w" id="mwBSo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBSs">M</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBSw">    </span><span class="o" id="mwBS0">*</span><span class="w" id="mwBS4"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBS8">TargetDistribution</span><span class="w" id="mwBTA"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBTE">(</span><span class="n" id="mwBTI">desired</span><span class="w" id="mwBTM"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBTQ">class</span><span class="w" id="mwBTU"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBTY">distribution</span><span class="w" id="mwBTc"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBTg">in</span><span class="w" id="mwBTk"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBTo">the</span><span class="w" id="mwBTs"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBTw">buffer</span><span class="p" id="mwBT0">)</span>

<span class="w" id="mwBT4">  </span><span class="n" id="mwBT8">Output</span><span class="o" id="mwBUA">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBUE"> </span>
<span class="w" id="mwBUI">    </span><span class="n" id="mwBUM">Updated</span><span class="w" id="mwBUQ"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBUU">buffer</span><span class="w" id="mwBUY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBUc">maintaining</span><span class="w" id="mwBUg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBUk">the</span><span class="w" id="mwBUo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBUs">target</span><span class="w" id="mwBUw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBU0">distribution</span>

<span class="w" id="mwBU4">  </span><span class="nb" id="mwBU8">Initialize</span><span class="w" id="mwBVA"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBVE">buffer</span><span class="w" id="mwBVI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBVM">M</span><span class="w" id="mwBVQ"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBVU">with</span><span class="w" id="mwBVY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBVc">the</span><span class="w" id="mwBVg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBVk">first</span><span class="w" id="mwBVo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBVs">M</span><span class="w" id="mwBVw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBV0">data</span><span class="w" id="mwBV4"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBV8">points</span><span class="w" id="mwBWA"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBWE">from</span><span class="w" id="mwBWI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBWM">the</span><span class="w" id="mwBWQ"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBWU">stream</span><span class="o" id="mwBWY">.</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBWc">  </span>
<span class="w" id="mwBWg">  </span><span class="k" id="mwBWk">For</span><span class="w" id="mwBWo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBWs">t</span><span class="w" id="mwBWw"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBW0">=</span><span class="w" id="mwBW4"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBW8">M</span><span class="o" id="mwBXA">+</span><span class="mi" id="mwBXE">1</span><span class="w" id="mwBXI"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBXM">to</span><span class="w" id="mwBXQ"> </span><span class="err" id="mwBXU">∞</span><span class="o" id="mwBXY">:</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBXc">    </span><span class="o" id="mwBXg">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBXk"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBXo">Update</span><span class="w" id="mwBXs"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBXw">stream</span><span class="w" id="mwBX0"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBX4">counters</span><span class="w" id="mwBX8"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBYA">to</span><span class="w" id="mwBYE"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBYI">compute</span><span class="w" id="mwBYM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBYQ">n_</span><span class="cm" id="mwBYU">{t+1}</span><span class="o" id="mwBYY">.</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBYc">    </span><span class="o" id="mwBYg">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBYk"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBYo">Compute</span><span class="w" id="mwBYs"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBYw">target</span><span class="w" id="mwBY0"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBY4">distribution</span><span class="w" id="mwBY8"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBZA">p_</span><span class="cm" id="mwBZE">{t+1}</span><span class="o" id="mwBZI">.</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBZM">    </span><span class="o" id="mwBZQ">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBZU"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBZY">For</span><span class="w" id="mwBZc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBZg">each</span><span class="w" id="mwBZk"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBZo">class</span><span class="w" id="mwBZs"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBZw">k</span><span class="w" id="mwBZ0"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBZ4">in</span><span class="w" id="mwBZ8"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBaA">the</span><span class="w" id="mwBaE"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBaI">buffer</span><span class="o" id="mwBaM">:</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBaQ">      </span><span class="o" id="mwBaU">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBaY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBac">Simulate</span><span class="w" id="mwBag"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBak">removing</span><span class="w" id="mwBao"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBas">one</span><span class="w" id="mwBaw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBa0">instance</span><span class="w" id="mwBa4"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBa8">of</span><span class="w" id="mwBbA"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBbE">class</span><span class="w" id="mwBbI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBbM">k</span><span class="w" id="mwBbQ"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBbU">and</span><span class="w" id="mwBbY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBbc">adding</span><span class="w" id="mwBbg"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBbk">(</span><span class="n" id="mwBbo">x_t</span><span class="o" id="mwBbs">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBbw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBb0">y_t</span><span class="p" id="mwBb4">)</span><span class="o" id="mwBb8">.</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBcA">      </span><span class="o" id="mwBcE">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBcI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBcM">Compute</span><span class="w" id="mwBcQ"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBcU">KL</span><span class="w" id="mwBcY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBcc">divergence</span><span class="w" id="mwBcg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBck">v_k</span><span class="w" id="mwBco"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBcs">between</span><span class="w" id="mwBcw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBc0">p_</span><span class="cm" id="mwBc4">{t+1}</span><span class="w" id="mwBc8"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBdA">and</span><span class="w" id="mwBdE"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBdI">the</span><span class="w" id="mwBdM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBdQ">modified</span><span class="w" id="mwBdU"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBdY">buffer</span><span class="w" id="mwBdc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBdg">distribution</span><span class="o" id="mwBdk">.</span>

<span class="w" id="mwBe4">    </span><span class="o" id="mwBe8">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBfA"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBfE">If</span><span class="w" id="mwBfI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBfM">k</span><span class="o" id="mwBfQ">*</span><span class="w" id="mwBfU"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBfY">=</span><span class="w" id="mwBfc"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBfg">y_t</span><span class="o" id="mwBfk">:</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBfo">      </span><span class="o" id="mwBfs">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBfw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBf0">Generate</span><span class="w" id="mwBf4"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwBf8">random</span><span class="w" id="mwBgA"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBgE">number</span><span class="w" id="mwBgI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBgM">r</span><span class="w" id="mwBgQ"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBgU">in</span><span class="w" id="mwBgY"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBgc">[</span><span class="mi" id="mwBgg">1</span><span class="o" id="mwBgk">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBgo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBgs">n_</span><span class="cm" id="mwBgw">{t+1}</span><span class="o" id="mwBg0">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBg4"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBg8">y_t</span><span class="p" id="mwBhA">]</span><span class="o" id="mwBhE">.</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBhI">      </span><span class="o" id="mwBhM">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBhQ"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBhU">If</span><span class="w" id="mwBhY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBhc">r</span><span class="w" id="mwBhg"> </span><span class="err" id="mwBhk">≤</span><span class="w" id="mwBho"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBhs">m_</span><span class="cm" id="mwBhw">{t, y_t}</span><span class="o" id="mwBh0">,</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBh4">        </span><span class="o" id="mwBh8">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBiA"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBiE">replace</span><span class="w" id="mwBiI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBiM">a</span><span class="w" id="mwBiQ"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwBiU">random</span><span class="w" id="mwBiY"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBic">instance</span><span class="w" id="mwBig"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBik">of</span><span class="w" id="mwBio"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBis">class</span><span class="w" id="mwBiw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBi0">y_t</span><span class="w" id="mwBi4"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBi8">in</span><span class="w" id="mwBjA"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBjE">the</span><span class="w" id="mwBjI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBjM">buffer</span><span class="w" id="mwBjQ"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBjU">with</span><span class="w" id="mwBjY"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBjc">(</span><span class="n" id="mwBjg">x_t</span><span class="o" id="mwBjk">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBjo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBjs">y_t</span><span class="p" id="mwBjw">)</span><span class="o" id="mwBj0">.</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBj4">    </span><span class="o" id="mwBj8">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBkA"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBkE">Else</span><span class="o" id="mwBkI">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBkM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBkQ">replace</span><span class="w" id="mwBkU"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBkY">a</span><span class="w" id="mwBkc"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwBkg">random</span><span class="w" id="mwBkk"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBko">instance</span><span class="w" id="mwBks"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBkw">of</span><span class="w" id="mwBk0"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBk4">the</span><span class="w" id="mwBk8"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBlA">selected</span><span class="w" id="mwBlE"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBlI">class</span><span class="w" id="mwBlM"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBlQ">k</span><span class="o" id="mwBlU">*</span><span class="w" id="mwBlY"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBlc">in</span><span class="w" id="mwBlg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBlk">the</span><span class="w" id="mwBlo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBls">buffer</span><span class="w" id="mwBlw"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBl0">with</span><span class="w" id="mwBl4"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBl8">(</span><span class="n" id="mwBmA">x_t</span><span class="o" id="mwBmE">,</span><span class="w" id="mwBmI"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBmM">y_t</span><span class="p" id="mwBmQ">)</span><span class="o" id="mwBmU">.</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBmY">    </span><span class="o" id="mwBmc">-</span><span class="w" id="mwBmg"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBmk">Update</span><span class="w" id="mwBmo"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBms">buffer</span><span class="w" id="mwBmw"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBm0">counters</span><span class="w" id="mwBm4"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBm8">m_</span><span class="cm" id="mwBnA">{t+1].</span>
<span class="cm" id="mwBnE">    - Update class set C_{t+1}</span><span class="w" id="mwBnI"> </span><span class="o" id="mwBnM">:=</span><span class="w" id="mwBnQ"> </span><span class="n" id="mwBnU">unique</span><span class="p" id="mwBnY">(</span><span class="n" id="mwBnc">C_t</span><span class="w" id="mwBng"> </span><span class="err" id="mwBnk">∪</span><span class="w" id="mwBno"> </span><span class="cm" id="mwBns">{y_t}</span><span class="p" id="mwBnw">)</span><span class="o" id="mwBn0">.</span>
</pre></div>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="15" id="mwBn4" aria-labelledby="Limitations"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Limitations">Limitations</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Limitations"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBn8">Reservoir sampling makes the assumption that the desired sample fits into <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Main_memory" title="Main memory" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBoA">main memory</a>, often implying that <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt184" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBoE" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"k"}},"i":0}}]}'>k</span> is a constant independent of <span class="texhtml mvar" style="font-style:italic;" about="#mwt185" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBoI" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"mvar","href":"./Template:Mvar"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"n"}},"i":0}}]}'>n</span>. In applications where we would like to select a large subset of the input list (say a third, i.e. <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt186" id="mwBoM" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k=n/3"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k=n/3}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>k</mi>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mo>/</mo>
        </mrow>
        <mn>3</mn>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k=n/3}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/783039bb5adcaeab56baf2ca269caa933de47a96" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:8.029ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k=n/3}"/></span>), other methods need to be adopted. Distributed implementations for this problem have been proposed.<sup about="#mwt187" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-17" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-17"}}'><a href="#cite_note-17" id="mwBoQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBoU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBoY">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBoc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="16" id="mwBog" aria-labelledby="References"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="References">References</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reservoir_sampling&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: References"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
